ADDED Wave-A scope: (a) native token-exchange/oauth2 auth provider + declarative schema + validation + expiry/refresh; (b) CrowdStrike auth-migration story; (c) crowdstrike-oauth2.prx retirement (regression surface — shipped via PLUGIN-MIGRATION-001-E; boot path + tests must migrate). | DRIFT-D923-002 [architect/PO-scope; D-923] | `validate_sensor_spec()` in prism-spec-engine performs syntactic and semantic validation of a loaded TOML sensor spec but is only called from test code (unit tests under `crates/prism-spec-engine/tests/`). The production spec-load path (`SpecEngine::load_sensor_spec()`) calls `parse_sensor_spec()` without calling `validate_sensor_spec()` afterward. This means a malformed or semantically-invalid TOML sensor spec (e.g., a table with duplicate column names, an invalid auth_type value, a negative timeout) can be loaded at runtime without triggering validation errors that would surface to the operator. The production impact depends on whether downstream code handles invalid spec state gracefully. Architect/PO adjudication needed: should `validate_sensor_spec()` be called inline in `load_sensor_spec()`, or should validation be a separate pre-load gate called by the orchestration layer? | Architect/PO adjudicate | separate disposition |
